  <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
      const languageLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.language-item[data-language-code]');
      const form = document.getElementById('language-form-js');
      const input = document.getElementById('language_code_input');

      languageLinks.forEach(link => {
        link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
          e.preventDefault();
          const languageCode = this.dataset.languageCode;
          input.value = languageCode;
          form.submit();
        });
      });
    });
  </script>

<script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    const modal = document.getElementById("customMenuModal");
    const openBtn = document.getElementById("openCustomModal");
    const closeBtns = document.querySelectorAll(".close-modal");

    if (openBtn) {
      openBtn.addEventListener("click", function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        modal.style.display = "block";
      });
    }

    closeBtns.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", function() {
        modal.style.display = "none";
      });
    });

    window.addEventListener("click", function(e) {
      if (e.target === modal) {
        modal.style.display = "none";
      }
    });
  });
</script>

  @media (min-width: 1600px) and (max-width:1799px) {

      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(370px, 1fr));
    }

  }

    .product-grid {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(236px, 1fr));
      gap: 15px;

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  // Variables globales
  let allProductsLoaded = {};
  let isLoadingProducts = {};

  // Función para parsear diferentes formatos de fecha
  function parseEventDate(dateString) {
    if (!dateString) return null;

    dateString = dateString.trim();

    // Mapa de meses en español y catalán
    const monthMap = {
      'enero': 1, 'febrero': 2, 'marzo': 3, 'abril': 4, 'mayo': 5, 'junio': 6,
      'julio': 7, 'agosto': 8, 'septiembre': 9, 'octubre': 10, 'noviembre': 11, 'diciembre': 12,
      'gener': 1, 'febrer': 2, 'març': 3, 'abril': 4, 'maig': 5, 'juny': 6,
      'juliol': 7, 'agost': 8, 'setembre': 9, 'octubre': 10, 'novembre': 11, 'desembre': 12
    };

    // Formato largo: "4 de enero de 2026" o "Domingo, 4 de enero de 2026"
    const longFormatMatch = dateString.match(/(\d{1,2})\s+de\s+(\w+)\s+de\s+(\d{4})/i);
    if (longFormatMatch) {
      const day = parseInt(longFormatMatch[1], 10);
      const monthName = longFormatMatch[2].toLowerCase();
      const year = parseInt(longFormatMatch[3], 10);
      const month = monthMap[monthName];
      if (month) {
        const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day);
        if (!isNaN(date.getTime())) return date;
      }
    }

    // Formatos cortos con punto o barra: "31.12.2025", "12/1/2026", "Miércoles, 31.12.2025"
    const shortFormatMatch = dateString.match(/(\d{1,2})[\.\/](\d{1,2})[\.\/](\d{4})/);
    if (shortFormatMatch) {
      const day = parseInt(shortFormatMatch[1], 10);
      const month = parseInt(shortFormatMatch[2], 10) - 1;
      const year = parseInt(shortFormatMatch[3], 10);
      const date = new Date(year, month, day);
      if (!isNaN(date.getTime())) return date;
    }

    return null;
}

// Obtiene fecha y hora actuales en la zona horaria de Madrid.
function getMadridNow() {
  const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
    timeZone: 'Europe/Madrid',
    hour12: false,
    year: 'numeric',
    month: '2-digit',
    day: '2-digit',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit'
  });

  const parts = formatter.formatToParts(new Date()).reduce(function(acc, part) {
    if (part.type !== 'literal') {
      acc[part.type] = parseInt(part.value, 10);
    }
    return acc;
  }, {});

  return {
    year: parts.year,
    month: parts.month,
    day: parts.day,
    hour: parts.hour,
    minute: parts.minute
  };
}

// Determina si el evento ya expiró en horario de Madrid (23:45 del mismo día).
function eventExpiredInMadrid(nowMadrid, eventDate) {
  if (!eventDate) return false;

  // Si el año actual es mayor, el evento ya pasó
  if (nowMadrid.year > eventDate.year) return true;
  if (nowMadrid.year < eventDate.year) return false;

  // Si el mes actual es mayor, el evento ya pasó
  if (nowMadrid.month > eventDate.month) return true;
  if (nowMadrid.month < eventDate.month) return false;

  // Si el día actual es mayor, el evento ya pasó
  if (nowMadrid.day > eventDate.day) return true;

  // Si el día actual es menor, el evento no ha pasado
  if (nowMadrid.day < eventDate.day) return false;

  // Si estamos en el mismo día, ocultar a las 23:45
  if (nowMadrid.hour > 23) return true;
  if (nowMadrid.hour < 23) return false;

  return nowMadrid.minute >= 45;
}

// Función para ordenar eventos
function sortEventsByDate() {
  const tabContents = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content');

  tabContents.forEach(function(tabContent) {
    const shouldSort = tabContent.dataset.sortEvents === 'true';
    const hidePast = tabContent.dataset.hidePast === 'true';

    const grid = tabContent.querySelector('.product-grid');
    if (!grid) return;

    const events = Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.product-item'));
    const nowMadrid = getMadridNow();

    // DEBUG: Log fecha actual de Madrid
    console.log('🕐 Fecha actual Madrid (grid):', nowMadrid);

    const eventsWithDates = events.map(function(event) {
      const dateText = event.dataset.eventDate || event.querySelector('.event-date-text')?.textContent;
      const parsedDate = parseEventDate(dateText);
      const dateParts = parsedDate ? {
        year: parsedDate.getFullYear(),
        month: parsedDate.getMonth() + 1,
        day: parsedDate.getDate()
      } : null;
      return {
        element: event,
        date: parsedDate,
        dateParts: dateParts,
        dateText: dateText
      };
    });

      if (shouldSort) {
        eventsWithDates.sort(function(a, b) {
          if (!a.date && !b.date) return 0;
          if (!a.date) return 1;
          if (!b.date) return -1;

          var dateDiff = a.date - b.date;
          if (dateDiff !== 0) return dateDiff;

          var titleA = (a.element.querySelector('.product-title a')?.textContent || '').toLowerCase();
          var titleB = (b.element.querySelector('.product-title a')?.textContent || '').toLowerCase();
          var aIsTardeo = titleA.includes('tardeo');
          var bIsTardeo = titleB.includes('tardeo');

          if (aIsTardeo && !bIsTardeo) return -1;
          if (!aIsTardeo && bIsTardeo) return 1;
          return 0;
        });
      }

      grid.innerHTML = '';

    let hiddenCount = 0;
    let shownCount = 0;

    eventsWithDates.forEach(function(item) {
      const isExpired = hidePast && eventExpiredInMadrid(nowMadrid, item.dateParts);

      if (isExpired) {
        console.log('❌ Ocultando evento pasado (grid):', item.dateText, item.dateParts);
        item.element.classList.add('past-event');
        hiddenCount++;
        return;
      }

      console.log('✅ Mostrando evento futuro (grid):', item.dateText, item.dateParts);
      item.element.classList.remove('past-event');
      grid.appendChild(item.element);
      shownCount++;
    });

    console.log(`📊 Grid - Eventos ocultos: ${hiddenCount}, Eventos mostrados: ${shownCount}`);
    });
  }

  // Función para traducir fechas en JavaScript
  function translateDate(dateStr, locale) {
    if (!dateStr || !locale) return dateStr;

    // Normalizar el día a Mayúscula Inicial sin importar cómo se tecleó
    let translated = dateStr.replace(/\b(monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sunday)\b/gi, function (w) {
      return w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase();
    });
    if (locale.includes('/es')) {
      translated = translated
        .replace('Monday', 'Lunes')
        .replace('Tuesday', 'Martes')
        .replace('Wednesday', 'Miércoles')
        .replace('Thursday', 'Jueves')
        .replace('Friday', 'Viernes')
        .replace('Saturday', 'Sábado')
        .replace('Sunday', 'Domingo');
    } else if (locale.includes('/ca')) {
      translated = translated
        .replace('Monday', 'Dilluns')
        .replace('Tuesday', 'Dimarts')
        .replace('Wednesday', 'Dimecres')
        .replace('Thursday', 'Dijous')
        .replace('Friday', 'Divendres')
        .replace('Saturday', 'Dissabte')
        .replace('Sunday', 'Diumenge');
    }
    return translated;
  }

  // Función para crear HTML de producto
  function createProductHTML(product, buyText, locale) {
    // Traducir la fecha
    const translatedDate = translateDate(product.event_date || '', locale);

    // Usar event_title si existe, si no usar title limpio
    let displayTitle = product.event_title || product.title || '';
    displayTitle = displayTitle.trim();
    if (!displayTitle) {
      displayTitle = product.title || '';
    }
    // Remover la fecha del título
    displayTitle = displayTitle.split(' - ')[0];

    return `
      <div class="product-item" data-event-date="${product.event_date || ''}">
        <a href="${product.url}">
           <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" id="Layer_1" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 66 66" style="
    position: absolute;
    left: 20px;
    top: 20px;
    border-radius: 100%;
    border: 1px solid #eee;
    width: 50px;
    height: 50px;
">
  <defs>
    <style>
      .st0 {
        fill: #fff;
        fill-rule: evenodd;
      }
    </style>
  </defs>
  <path class="st0" d="M29.4,26.7v1.8c0,.2,0,.5-.3.6-.2.2-.4.3-.6.3s-.5,0-.6-.3c-.2-.2-.3-.4-.3-.6v-1.8c0-.2,0-.5.3-.6s.4-.3.6-.3.5,0,.6.3c.2.2.3.4.3.6Z"></path>
  <path class="st0" d="M29.4,32.1v1.8c0,.2,0,.5-.3.6-.2.2-.4.3-.6.3s-.5,0-.6-.3c-.2-.2-.3-.4-.3-.6v-1.8c0-.2,0-.5.3-.6.2-.2.4-.3.6-.3s.5,0-.6.3c.2.2.3.4.3.6Z"></path>
  <path class="st0" d="M29.4,37.5v1.8c0,.2,0,.5-.3.6-.2.2-.4.3-.6.3s-.5,0-.6-.3-.3-.4-.3-.6v-1.8c0-.2,0-.5.3-.6.2-.2.4-.3.6-.3s.5,0-.6.3c.2.2.3.4.3.6Z"></path>
  <path class="st0" d="M33,0C14.8,0,0,14.8,0,33s14.8,33,33,33,33-14.8,33-33S51.2,0,33,0ZM47.5,29.4c-1,0-1.8.4-2.6,1.1-.7.7-1.1,1.6-1.1,2.6s.4,1.8,1.1,2.6c.7.7,1.6,1.1,2.6,1.1v5.4c0,.5-.2.9-.5,1.3-.4.3-.8.5-1.3.5h-16.3v-.9c0-.2,0-.5-.3-.6-.2-.2-.4-.3-.6-.3s-.5,0-.6.3c-.2.2-.3.4-.3.6v.9h-7.2c-.5,0-.9-.2-1.3-.5-.4-.4-.5-.8-.5-1.3v-5.4c1,0,1.9-.4,2.6-1.1.7-.7,1.1-1.6,1.1-2.6s-.4-1.8-1.1-2.6c-.7-.7-1.6-1.1-2.6-1.1v-5.4c0-.5.2-.9.5-1.3.4-.3.8-.5,1.3-.5h7.2v.9c0,.2,0,.5.3.6.2.2.4.3.6.3s.5,0-.6-.3c.2-.2.3-.4.3-.6v-.9h16.3c.5,0,.9.2,1.3.5.3.4.5.8.5,1.3v5.4Z"></path>
</svg>
          <img src="${product.featured_image}" alt="${displayTitle}">
        </a>
        <p style="color:#fff;margin:15px 0px 0px;" class="event-date-text">${translatedDate}</p>
        <div class="product-title">
          <a href="${product.url}">${displayTitle}</a>
        </div>
        <div class="product-details">
         <a href="${product.url}"><button type="submit" class="buy-now-btn">${buyText}</button></a>
          <span class="wishlist-icon">♡</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  // Función para cargar más productos vía AJAX
  async function loadMoreProducts(tabContent) {
    const tabId = tabContent.id;
    const tabIndex = tabId.replace('tab', '');

    console.log(`🔄 Cargando más productos para ${tabId}`);

    if (isLoadingProducts[tabId] || allProductsLoaded[tabId]) {
      return;
    }

    const collectionHandle = tabContent.dataset.collectionHandle;
    const buyText = tabContent.dataset.buyText;
    const locale = tabContent.dataset.locale;
    const loadingSpinner = document.getElementById(`loading-${tabIndex}`);

    const totalProducts = parseInt(tabContent.dataset.totalProducts);
    const currentPage = parseInt(tabContent.dataset.currentPage);

    if (!totalProducts || totalProducts <= 50) {
      allProductsLoaded[tabId] = true;
      return;
    }

    const realTotalPages = Math.ceil(totalProducts / 50);

    if (currentPage >= realTotalPages) {
      allProductsLoaded[tabId] = true;
      return;
    }

    isLoadingProducts[tabId] = true;
    if (loadingSpinner) {
      loadingSpinner.style.display = 'block';
    }

    const grid = tabContent.querySelector('.product-grid');
    let productsAdded = 0;

    try {
      for (let page = currentPage + 1; page <= realTotalPages; page++) {
        console.log(`📄 Cargando página ${page} de ${realTotalPages}`);

        // Intentar primero con el endpoint JSON personalizado
        const jsonUrl = `/collections/${collectionHandle}?page=${page}&view=json`;
        const response = await fetch(jsonUrl);

        if (response.ok) {
          try {
            const data = await response.json();
            if (data.products) {
              data.products.forEach(product => {
                const productHTML = createProductHTML({
                  url: product.url,
                  featured_image: product.featured_image,
                  title: product.title,
                  event_date: product.event_date,
                  event_title: product.event_title
                }, buyText, locale);

                const tempDiv = document.createElement('div');
                tempDiv.innerHTML = productHTML;
                grid.appendChild(tempDiv.firstElementChild);
                productsAdded++;
              });
            }
          } catch (parseError) {
            // Si no es JSON, intentar como HTML
            const html = await response.text();
            const parser = new DOMParser();
            const doc = parser.parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
            const products = doc.querySelectorAll('.product-item');

            products.forEach(product => {
              const clonedProduct = product.cloneNode(true);
              grid.appendChild(clonedProduct);
              productsAdded++;
            });
          }
        } else {
          // Fallback: endpoint nativo de Shopify
          const altUrl = `/collections/${collectionHandle}/products.json?page=${page}&limit=50`;
          const altResponse = await fetch(altUrl);

          if (altResponse.ok) {
            const data = await altResponse.json();
            if (data.products) {
              data.products.forEach(product => {
                const productHTML = createProductHTML({
                  url: `/products/${product.handle}`,
                  featured_image: product.images && product.images[0] ? product.images[0].src : '',
                  title: product.title,
                  event_date: '',
                  event_title: ''
                }, buyText, locale);

                const tempDiv = document.createElement('div');
                tempDiv.innerHTML = productHTML;
                grid.appendChild(tempDiv.firstElementChild);
                productsAdded++;
              });
            }
          }
        }
      }

      console.log(`✅ Total de productos agregados: ${productsAdded}`);
      tabContent.dataset.currentPage = realTotalPages;
      allProductsLoaded[tabId] = true;

      // Re-ordenar después de cargar todos los productos
      sortEventsByDate();

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error cargando productos:', error);
    } finally {
      isLoadingProducts[tabId] = false;
      if (loadingSpinner) {
        loadingSpinner.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
  }

  // Ejecutar el ordenamiento al cargar la página
  sortEventsByDate();

  // Cargar más productos si está habilitado
  const activeTab = document.querySelector('.tab-content.active');
  if (activeTab && activeTab.dataset.loadAll === 'true') {
    loadMoreProducts(activeTab);
  }

  // Configurar tabs
  const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#product-tabs li');
  const contents = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content');
  const viewMoreBtn = document.getElementById('view-more-button');

  tabs.forEach(tab => {
    tab.addEventListener('click', function() {
      tabs.forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
      contents.forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));

      this.classList.add('active');
      const activeTab = document.getElementById(this.dataset.tab);
      activeTab.classList.add('active');

      const viewMoreLink = activeTab.getAttribute('data-view-more');
      if (viewMoreLink) {
        viewMoreBtn.href = viewMoreLink;
      }

      // Re-ordenar eventos cuando se cambia de tab
      setTimeout(sortEventsByDate, 10);

      // Cargar más productos si es necesario
      if (activeTab.dataset.loadAll === 'true' && !allProductsLoaded[activeTab.id]) {
        loadMoreProducts(activeTab);
      }
    });
  });

  // Configurar el enlace "View More" inicial
  if (activeTab) {
    const link = activeTab.getAttribute('data-view-more');
    if (link) {
      viewMoreBtn.href = link;
    }
  }
});
</script>

  <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
      const footerLanguageLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.footer-language-item[data-language-code]');
      const footerForm = document.getElementById('footer-language-form-js');
      const footerInput = document.getElementById('footer_language_code_input');

      if (footerForm && footerInput) {
        footerLanguageLinks.forEach(link => {
          link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const languageCode = this.dataset.languageCode;
            footerInput.value = languageCode;
            footerForm.submit();
          });
        });
      }
    });

    <!-- Script -->
    <script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const menuBtn = document.querySelector('.menu-button');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('menu-close');
    const overlay = document.getElementById('fullscreen-menu');

    function openMenu(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      overlay.classList.add('active');
      overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }

    function closeMenu() {
      overlay.classList.remove('active');
      overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }

    if (!overlay) return;

    if (menuBtn) menuBtn.addEventListener('click', openMenu);
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
    overlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      if (e.target === overlay) closeMenu();
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') closeMenu();
    });
  });
</script>
